A JSON object that can combine explicit paths with keyword/content searches.\n\nJSON example: { \"paths\": [\"src/constants\"], \"root\": \"src\", \"searches\": [{ \"include\": [\"history\", \"progress\"], \"match\": \"path-or-content\", \"includeMode\": \"any\" }], \"exclude\": [\"(?:^|/)(?:node_modules|dist|out|build|coverage|\\.next|\\.git)(?:/|$)\"], \"regex\": false }\n\nsearches[] groups are ORed and deduplicated with paths[]. includeMode is any (OR) or all (AND). match is path, content, path-or-content, or path-and-content. Literal matching is case-insensitive unless regex is enabled. Group root/exclude/regex may override/narrow global settings.\n\nPrefer exact paths when known. \"../../../../shared/ui/reference-model/reference-model-viewer\";\nimport { getGraphPresentationState } from \"../../../../foundation/reference-data/model/graph-presentation-state\";\nimport type { getAuditorGraphFilters,\n  getAuditorStatusFilter,\n  setAuditorCheckFilter,\n  setAuditorGraphFilters,\n  setAuditorStatusFilter,\n} from \"../../auditor-ui-settings\";\nimport { interpretResolveState } from \"../../checks/reference-resolved-check\";\nimport { downloadAuditFindingsCsv } from \"../../export/audit-csv-exporter\";\nimport { AuditorGrid } from \"../auditor-grid/auditor-grid\";\nimport type { AuditorTestDetailsSelection } from \"../../auditor-grid/auditor-grid.types.ts\";\nimport { AuditorHelpDialog } from \"../auditor-help-dialog/auditor-help-dialog\";\nimport { AuditorTestDetailsDialog } from \"../auditor-test-details-dialog/auditor-test-details-dialog\";\nimport { AuditorSummary } from \"../auditor-summary/auditor-summary\";\nimport { AuditorToolbar } from \"../auditor-toolbar/auditor-toolbar\";\nimport { NodeDetailsPanel } from \"../../../../shared/ui/details/node-details-panel\";\nimport { FoblesDetailsWindow } from \"../../../../shared/dialog/fobles-details-window\";\nconst ALL_STATUSES: readonly AuditFindingStatus[] = [\"pending\", \"passed\", \"warning\", \"error\"];\nconst DATA_OPERATION_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 100;\nconst DEFAULT_GRAPH_FILTERS: SitecoreHarvestFiltersState = {\n  parent: false,\n  children: false,\n  layouts: true,\n  referrers: false,\n  customFields: false,\n  standardFields: false,\n  template: false,\n};\n\nexport type AuditorAppProps = { doc: Document; findingStore: AuditFindingStore; workOperationId: string };\n\ntype FoblesDataOperationHandle = ReturnType<typeof getFoblesDataOperation>;\n// AI NOTE: The Auditor dialog opens while the audit run is still acquiring the Reference Model, so the Data\n// AI NOTE: Service operation does not exist yet at mount. Wait for it instead of subscribing once and giving up.\nfunction useFoblesDataOperationWhenReady(workOperationId: string): FoblesDataOperationHandle {\n  const [operation, setOperation] = useState<FoblesDataOperationHandle>(() => getFoblesDataOperation(workOperationId));\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    const startedAt = performance.now();\n    function attach(): boolean {\n      const found = getFoblesDataOperation(workOperationId);\n      if (!found || !active) {\n        return false;\n      }\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Auditor dialog: data operation attached\", {\n        workOperationId,\n        waitedMs: Math.round(performance.now() - startedAt),\n      });\n      setOperation(found);\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (attach()) {\n      return () => {\n        active = false;\n      };\n    }\n    const timer = window.setInterval(() => {\n      if (attach()) {\n        window.clearInterval(timer);\n      }\n    }, DATA_OPERATION_POLL_INTERVAL_MS);\n    return () => {\n      active = false;\n      window.clearInterval(timer);\n    };\n  }, [workOperationId]);\n  return operation;\n}\n\nexport function AuditorApp({ doc, findingStore, workOperationId }: AuditorAppProps) {\n  const [snapshot, setSnapshot] = useState<readonly AuditFinding[]>(() => findingStore.getSnapshot());\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(\"\");\n  const [modelRevision, setModelRevision] = useState(0);\n  const [harvestProgress, setHarvestProgress] = useState<PhaseProgress>();\n  const [acquisitionProgress, setAcquisitionProgress] = useState<FoblesAcquisitionProgress>();\n  const [liveResolveProgress, setLiveResolveProgress] = useState<PhaseProgress>();\n  const [selectedStatuses, setSelectedStatuses] = useState<ReadonlySet<AuditFindingStatus>>(\n    () => new Set(ALL_STATUSES),\n  );\n  const [selectedChecks, setSelectedChecks] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(() => new Set());\n  const [graphFilters, setGraphFilters] = useState<SitecoreHarvestFiltersState>(DEFAULT_GRAPH_FILTERS);\n  const [filtersLoaded, setFiltersLoaded] = useState(false);\n  const [viewReferenceModel, setViewReferenceModel] = useState(false);\n  const [selectedTreeNodeId, setSelectedTreeNodeId] = useState<string>();\n  const [selectedTestDetails, setSelectedTestDetails] = useState<AuditorTestDetailsSelection | undefined>(undefined);\n  const [showHelp, setShowHelp] = useState(false);\n  const appRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const startedAt = useRef(performance.now());\n  const completedElapsedSeconds = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);\n  const dataOperation = useFoblesDataOperationWhenReady(workOperationId);\n  const data = dataOperation?.data;\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filtersLoaded) {\n      dataOperation?.updateFilters(graphFilters);\n    }\n  }, [dataOperation, filtersLoaded, graphFilters]);\n  const model = data?.model;\n  useEffect(() => findingStore.subscribe(setSnapshot), [findingStore]);\n  // AI NOTE: A cold item runs a live harvest before any data operation exists. Subscribe by work-operation id up front so\n  // AI NOTE: that harvest's progress is visible instead of an empty dialog.\n  useEffect(\n    () =>\n      subscribeFoblesAcquisitionProgress(workOperationId, (progress) => {\n        extensionLog.debug(\"Auditor dialog: acquisition progress received\", { workOperationId, progress });\n        setAcquisitionProgress(progress);\n      }),\n    [workOperationId],\n  );\n  // AI NOTE: Persisting is gated on filtersLoaded so the initial defaults never overwrite the stored values.\n  // AI NOTE: Graph filters fall back to the Graph's stored filters until the Auditor has its own stored set.\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    void Promise.all([\n      getAuditorStatusFilter(),\n      getAuditorCheckFilter(),\n      getAuditorGraphFilters(),\n      getReferenceGraphFilters(),\n    ]).then(([storedStatuses, storedChecks, storedGraphFilters, referenceGraphFilters]) => {\n      if (!active) {\n        return;\n      }\n      if (storedStatuses) {\n        setSelectedStatuses(new Set(ALL_STATUSES.filter((status) => storedStatuses.includes(status))));\n      }\n      if (storedChecks) {\n        setSelectedChecks(new Set(storedChecks));\n      }\n      setGraphFilters({ ...referenceGraphFilters, ...storedGraphFilters });\n      setFiltersLoaded(true);\n    });\n    return () => {\n      active = false;\n    };\n  }, []);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filtersLoaded) {\n      void setAuditorStatusFilter(Array.from(selectedStatuses));\n    }\n  }, [filtersLoaded, selectedStatuses]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filtersLoaded) {\n      void setAuditorCheckFilter(Array.from(selectedChecks));\n    }\n  }, [filtersLoaded, selectedChecks]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (filtersLoaded) {\n      void setAuditorGraphFilters(graphFilters);\n    }\n  }, [filtersLoaded, graphFilters]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!dataOperation) {\n      return;\n    }\n    const unsubscribeChange = dataOperation.subscribeChange(() => {\n      setModelRevision((current) => current + 1);\n    });\n    const unsubscribeHarvest = dataOperation.subscribeHarvestProgress((progress) => {\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Auditor dialog: harvest progress received\", { workOperationId, progress });\n      setHarvestProgress(progress);\n    });\n    const unsubscribeProcessing = dataOperation.subscribeProcessingProgress((progress) => {\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Auditor dialog: processing progress received\", {\n        workOperationId,\n        completedItemCount: progress.completedItemCount,\n        maxItemCount: progress.maxItemCount,\n        status: progress.status,\n      });\n      setLiveResolveProgress({\n        completed: progress.completedItemCount,\n        max: progress.maxItemCount,\n        status: progress.status,\n      });\n    });\n    return () => {\n      unsubscribeChange();\n      unsubscribeHarvest();\n      unsubscribeProcessing();\n    };\n  }, [dataOperation, workOperationId]);\n  // AI NOTE: Fallback that needs no event replay: count unique item GUIDs whose every Resolve candidate node\n  // AI NOTE: has reached a terminal processing state, the same unit Graph's Resolving bar counts.\n  const derivedResolveProgress = useMemo<PhaseProgress | undefined>(() => {\n    if (!data || !model) {\n      return undefined;\n    }\n    const treeNodeIdsByGuid = new Map<string, string[]>();\n    data.resolveCandidateTreeNodeIds.forEach((treeNodeId) => {\n      const node = model.nodesById.get(treeNodeId);\n      const guid = (node?.nodeType === \"item\" ? node.scItem.scItemId || treeNodeId : treeNodeId).toLowerCase();\n      const treeNodeIds = treeNodeIdsByGuid.get(guid) ?? [];\n      treeNodeIds.push(treeNodeId);\n      treeNodeIdsByGuid.set(guid, treeNodeIds);\n    });\n    let completed = 0;\n    treeNodeIdsByGuid.forEach((treeNodeIds) => {\n      const terminal = treeNodeIds.every((treeNodeId) => {\n        const status = model.processingStateByTreeNodeId.get(treeNodeId);\n        return status !== undefined && status !== \"pending\" && status !== \"enriching\";\n      });\n      if (terminal) {\n        completed += 1;\n      }\n    });\n    const max = treeNodeIdsByGuid.size;\n    return { completed, max, status: completed >= max ? \"complete\" : \"running\" };\n  }, [data, model, modelRevision]);\n  const resolveProgress = liveResolveProgress ?? derivedResolveProgress;\n  if (resolveProgress?.status === \"complete\" && completedElapsedSeconds.current === undefined) {\n    completedElapsedSeconds.current = (performance.now() - startedAt.current) / 1000;\n  }\n  const resolveComplete = resolveProgress?.status === \"complete\";\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const showHelpFromWindow = (): void => setShowHelp(true);\n    window.addEventListener(\"fobles:auditor-help\", showHelpFromWindow);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"fobles:auditor-help\", showHelpFromWindow);\n  }, []);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    logHeapSnapshot(\"auditor-opened\", { workOperationId });\n  }, [workOperationId]);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (resolveComplete) {\n      logHeapSnapshot(\"auditor-resolve-complete\", { workOperationId });\n    }\n  }, [resolveComplete, workOperationId]);\n  const projectedFindings = useMemo(() => {\n    const nodesById = model?.nodesById;\n    return snapshot.map((finding) => {\n      const node = finding.referenceModelNodeId ? nodesById?.get(finding.referenceModelNodeId) : undefined;\n      if (!node) {\n        return finding;\n      }\n      const presentation = getGraphPresentationState(node);\n      const processingStatus = model?.processingStateByTreeNodeId.get(node.treeNodeId);\n      const liveResolve =\n        finding.checkKey === AUDIT_CHECK.REFERENCE_RESOLVED.KEY ? interpretResolveState(processingStatus) : undefined;\n      return {\n        ...finding,\n        dataType: presentation.kind,\n        status: liveResolve?.status ?? finding.status,\n        summary: liveResolve?.summary ?? finding.summary,\n      };\n    });\n  }, [snapshot, model, modelRevision]);\n  const filterableFindings = useMemo(() => {\n    const nodesById = model?.nodesById;\n    const normalizedQuery = query.trim().toLowerCase();\n    return projectedFindings.filter((finding) => {\n      if (selectedChecks.size > 0 && !selectedChecks.has(finding.checkKey)) {\n        return false;\n      }\n      const node = finding.referenceModelNodeId ? nodesById?.get(finding.referenceModelNodeId) : undefined;\n      const branch = node ? getGraphPresentationState(node).branch : undefined;\n      if (branch && !graphFilters[branch]) {\n        return false;\n      }\n      if (!normalizedQuery) {\n        return true;\n      }\n      const searchable = [\n        finding.dataType,\n        finding.itemId,\n        finding.itemName,\n        finding.itemPath,\n        finding.checkLabel,\n        finding.context,\n        finding.summary,\n        finding.details,\n      ]\n        .filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value))\n        .join(\"\\n\")\n        .toLowerCase();\n      return searchable.includes(normalizedQuery);\n    });\n  }, [projectedFindings, selectedChecks, query, graphFilters, model]);\n  const visibleFindings = useMemo(\n    () => filterableFindings.filter((finding) => selectedStatuses.has(finding.status)),\n    [filterableFindings, selectedStatuses],\n  );\n  const summary = useMemo(() => {\n    const counts = { pending: 0, passed: 0, warning: 0, error: 0, total: filterableFindings.length };\n    filterableFindings.forEach((finding) => {\n      counts[finding.status] += 1;\n    });\n    return counts;\n  }, [filterableFindings]);\n  function toggleStatus(status: AuditFindingStatus): void {\n    setSelectedStatuses((current) => {\n      const next = new Set(current);\n      if (next.has(status)) {\n        next.delete(status);\n      } else {\n        next.add(status);\n      }\n      return next;\n    });\n  }\n  function toggleCheck(checkKey: string): void {\n    setSelectedChecks((current) => {\n      const next = new Set(current);\n      if (next.has(checkKey)) {\n        next.delete(checkKey);\n      } else {\n        next.add(checkKey);\n      }\n      return next;\n    });\n  }\n  function resetFilters(): void {\n    setQuery(\"\");\n    setSelectedStatuses(new       selectedChecks={selectedChecks}\n        onToggleCheck={toggleCheck}\n        onReset={resetFilters}\n        onExport={exportCsv}\n        graphFilters={graphFilters}\n        onGraphFilterChange={(key, checked) => {\n          setGraphFilters((current) => ({ ...current, [key]: checked }));\n        }}\n        onClearGraphFilters={() => {\n          setGraphFilters(\n            (current) =>\n              Object.fromEntries(\n                Object.keys(current).map((key) => [key, false]),\n              ) as unknown as SitecoreHarvestFiltersState,\n          );\n        }}\n        onViewReferenceModel={() => setViewReferenceModel(true)}\n        footer={\n          <LifecycleProgressFooter\n            harvestProgress={\n              harvestProgress ?? (\n            <AuditorGrid\n              referenceModelRoot={model.referenceModel}\n              findings={visibleFindings}\n              processingStateByTreeNodeId={model.processingStateByTreeNodeId}\n              modelRevision={modelRevision}\n              graphFilters={graphFilters}\n              onViewData={setSelectedTreeNodeId}\n              onViewTestDetails={setSelectedTestDetails}\n            />\n          ) : null}\n        </div>\n        {selectedTreeNodeId && model ?       "content": "// Proxy Buttons mirror a real Sitecore ribbon checkbox and proxy clicks to it. The ribbon\n// checkbox stays the source of truth. These live in the main toolbar (not the editor\n// header) since the editor header gets redrawn every time a tree item is picked.\nimport { getChildFrameDocuments } from \"./shared/frame-documents\";\n\nexport const findRibbonCheckbox = (doc: Document, checkboxId: string): HTMLInputElement | null => {\n  const ownCheckbox = doc.getElementById(checkboxId) as HTMLInputElement | null;\n  const frameCheckbox = ownCheckbox\n    ? null\n    : (getChildFrameDocuments(doc)\n        .map((frameDoc) => findRibbonCheckbox(frameDoc, checkboxId))\n        .find((result): result is HTMLInputElement => result !== null) ?? null);\n  return ownCheckbox ?? frameCheckbox;\n};\n\nexport const postSitecoreEvent = (doc: Document, eventName: string): boolean => {\n  const host = doc.body;\n  if (!host) {\n    return false;\n  }\n\n  const trigger = doc.createElement(\"button\");\n  trigger.type = \"button\";\n  trigger.hidden = true;\n  trigger.setAttribute(\n    \"onclick\",\n    `if (typeof scForm !== 'undefined') { return scForm.postEvent(this, event, '${eventName}'); }`,\n  );\n  host.appendChild(trigger);\n  trigger.click();\n  trigger.remove();\n  return true;\n};\n"

      "encoding": "utf8",
      "content": "import { STORAGE } from \"../../../constants/extension-const\";\nimport { CONST } from \"../../../constants/const\";\nimport { extensionLog } from \"../../logger\";\n\nconst KICK_ALL_USERS_WAIT_MS = 1_000;\nconst KICK_ALL_USERS_MAX_BUTTON_RETRIES = 5;\nconst KICK_ALL_USERS_BUTTON_RETRY_KEY = `${STORAGE.KEY.KICK_ALL_USERS}_button_retries`;\n\nconst clearButtonRetryCount = (): void => {\n  localStorage.removeItem(KICK_ALL_USERS_BUTTON_RETRY_KEY);\n};\n\nconst giveUpKickAllUsers = (message: string): void => {\n  localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE.KEY.KICK_ALL_USERS);\n  clearButtonRetryCount();\n  extensionLog.error(message);\n};\n\n// Walls off the \"count this failure, give up after too many, otherwise reschedule\" bookkeeping\n// so the two call sites below only describe *what* failed, not *how* retries are tracked.\nconst retryOrGiveUp = (doc: Document, warnMessage: string, giveUpMessage: string): void => {\n  const retries = Number(localStorage.getItem(KICK_ALL_USERS_BUTTON_RETRY_KEY) ?? \"0\") + 1;\n  localStorage.setItem(KICK_ALL_USERS_BUTTON_RETRY_KEY, String(retries));\n  extensionLog.warn(warnMessage, { retries, maxRetries: KICK_ALL_USERS_MAX_BUTTON_RETRIES });\n\n  if (retries >= KICK_ALL_USERS_MAX_BUTTON_RETRIES) {\n    giveUpKickAllUsers(giveUpMessage);\n  } else {\n    window.setTimeout(() => {\n      void processKickAllUsers(doc);\n    }, KICK_ALL_USERS_WAIT_MS);\n  }\n};\n\nconst getKickUsersUrl = (doc: Document): string => {\n  const origin = doc.defaultView?.location.origin ?? window.location.origin;\n  return `${origin}${CONST.SITECORE.RELATIVE_PATHS_ENCODED.KICK_USERS}`;\n};\n\nconst findKickButton = (doc: Document): HTMLButtonElement | null =>\n  Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>(\"button\")).find(\n    (button) => button.textContent?.trim().toLowerCase() === \"kick off user\",\n  ) ?? \"unknown user\",\n    className: row.className,\n    attributes: Array.from(row.attributes).map((attribute) => ({ name: attribute.name, value: attribute.value })),\n    outerHtml: row.outerHTML.slice(0, 1_000),\n  });\n\n  const userCell = row.cells.item(0);\n  if (!userCell) {\n    extensionLog.warn(\"Kick All Users row has no user-name cell\");\n    return;\n  }\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users user cell object before selection\", userCell);\n  userCell.click();\n  await new Promise((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, KICK_ALL_USERS_WAIT_MS));\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users user cell object after selection\", userCell);\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users row object after selection\", row);\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users selected row state\", {\n    user: row.cells.item(0)?.textContent?.trim() ?? \"unknown user\",\n    className: row.className,\n    attributes: Array.from(row.attributes).map((attribute) => ({ name: attribute.name, value: attribute.value })),\n    outerHtml: row.outerHTML.slice(0, 1_000),\n  });\n\n  const kickButton = findKickButton(doc);\n  if (!kickButton) {\n    retryOrGiveUp(\n      doc,\n      \"Kick All Users could not find Kick off user button\",\n      \"Kick All Users stopped after repeated missing buttons\",\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users button state\", {\n    disabledProperty: kickButton.disabled,\n    disabledAttribute: kickButton.getAttribute(\"disabled\"),\n    className: kickButton.className,\n    dataScClick: kickButton.getAttribute(\"data-sc-click\"),\n    outerHtml: kickButton.outerHTML.slice(0, 500),\n  });\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users button object before confirmation\", kickButton);\n\n  if (kickButton.disabled) {\n    retryOrGiveUp(\n      doc,\n      \"Kick All Users button is disabled; waiting\",\n      \"Kick All Users stopped after repeated disabled buttons\",\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  clearButtonRetryCount();\n  const userName = row.cells.item(0)?.textContent?.trim() ?? \"unknown user\";\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users about to ask for confirmation\", { user: userName, remainingRows: rows.length });\n  if (!window.confirm(`Kick off Sitecore user \"${userName}\"?`)) {\n    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE.KEY.KICK_ALL_USERS);\n    extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users cancelled\", { user: userName });\n    return;\n  }\n\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users clicking button\", {\n    user: userName,\n    disabledProperty: kickButton.disabled,\n    disabledAttribute: kickButton.getAttribute(\"disabled\"),\n    outerHtml: kickButton.outerHTML.slice(0, 500),\n  });\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users kicking row\", { user: userName, remainingRows: rows.length });\n  kickButton.click();\n  window.setTimeout(() => {\n    if (localStorage.getItem(STORAGE.KEY.KICK_ALL_USERS) === \"1\") {\n      window.location.assign(getKickUsersUrl(doc));\n    }\n  }, KICK_ALL_USERS_WAIT_MS * 2);\n};\n\nexport const kickAllUsers = (doc: Document): void => {\n  localStorage.setItem(STORAGE.KEY.KICK_ALL_USERS, \"1\");\n  const kickUsersUrl = getKickUsersUrl(doc);\n  extensionLog.info(\"Kick All Users opening worker tab\", { url: kickUsersUrl });\n  window.open(kickUsersUrl, \"_blank\", \"noopener,noreferrer\");\n};\n\nexport function resumeKickAllUsers(doc: Document): void {\n  const path = new URL(doc.defaultView?.location.href ?? window.location.href).pathname;\n  if (path.toLowerCase() === CONST.SITECORE.RELATIVE_PATHS_ENCODED.KICK_USERS.toLowerCase()) {\n    void processKickAllUsers(doc);\n  }\n}\n"

FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN * 2);\n  const maxHeight = Math.max(minHeight, viewport.height - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN * 2);\n  const width = Math.min(maxWidth, Math.max(minWidth, rect.width));\n  const height = Math.min(maxHeight, Math.max(minHeight, rect.height));\n  const left = Math.min(\n    viewport.width - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN - width,\n    Math.max(FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN, rect.left),\n  );\n  const top = Math.min(\n    viewport.height - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN - FOBLES_WINDOW_TITLE_BAR_HEIGHT,\n    Math.max(FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN, rect.top),\n  );\n  return { left, top, width, height };\n}\n\nexport function centeredRect(\n  win: Window,\n  width: number,\n  height: number,\n  minWidth: number,\n  minHeight: number,\n): WindowRect {\n  const viewport = viewportRect(win);\n  return clampRect(\n    win,\n    { left: Math.round((viewport.width - width) / 2), top: Math.round((viewport.height - height) / 2), width, height },\n    minWidth,\n    minHeight,\n  );\n}\n\nexport function readRect(element: HTMLElement): WindowRect {\n  const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();\n  return { left: rect.left, top: rect.top, width: rect.width, height: rect.height };\n}\n\n// Validates and clamps a raw persisted-storage value into a live-ready partial state, or returns\n// undefined if the record isn't usable (wrong shape, foreign discriminator, non-finite numbers).\n// Clamping here (not just on the next drag/resize) prevents a stale/corrupt/off-viewport record\n// from rendering the window with a negative or oversized geometry before any further interaction.\nexport function parsePersistedFoblesWindowState(\n  stored: unknown,\n  win: Window,\n  minWidth: number,\n  minHeight: number,\n): Partial<FoblesWindowState> | undefined {\n  if (!stored || typeof stored !== \"object\") {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  const candidate = stored as Partial<FoblesWindowState>;\n  if (candidate.type !== undefined && candidate.type !== FOBLES_WINDOW_STATE_TYPE) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  const hasValidRect = [candidate.left, candidate.top, candidate.width, candidate.height].every(\n    (value) => typeof value === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(value),\n  );\n  if (!hasValidRect) {\n    return undefined;\n  }\n  const clamped = clampRect(\n    win,\n    {\n      left: candidate.left as number,\n      top: candidate.top as number,\n      width: candidate.width as number,\n      height: candidate.height as number,\n    },\n    minWidth,\n    minHeight,\n  );\n  return { ...clamped, collapsed: candidate.collapsed === true, maximized: candidate.maximized === true };\n}\n"

STACK_STORAGE_KEY = \"fobles.window.stack\";\nconst WINDOW_SELECTOR = \".fobles-window[data-fobles-window-key]\";\nconst WINDOW_STACK_BASE_Z_INDEX = 101;\nconst COLLAPSED_LEFT_PROPERTY = PersistedWindowStack = { order: string[] };\n\nfunction mountedWindows(): HTMLElement[] {\n  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(WINDOW_SELECTOR));\n}\n\nfunction windowKey(element: HTMLElement): string {\n  return element.dataset.foblesWindowKey ?? \"\";\n}\n\nfunction currentZIndex(element: HTMLElement): number {\n  const value = Number.parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(element).zIndex, 10);\n  return Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0;\n}\n\nfunction persistOrder(windows: readonly HTMLElement[]): void {\n  void setStorageValue({ [STACK_STORAGE_KEY]: { order: windows.map(windowKey).filter(Boolean) } satisfies PersistedWindowStack }, \"local\");\n}\n\nfunction normalize(windows: readonly HTMLElement[]): void expanded = windows.filter((element) => !element.classList.contains(\"is-collapsed\"));\n  expanded.forEach((element) => {\n    delete element.dataset[COLLAPSED_ORDER_PROPERTY];\n  });\n  const collapsed = windows.filter((element) => element.classList.contains(\"is-collapsed\"));\n  collapsed.forEach((element) => {\n    if (element.dataset[COLLAPSED_ORDER_PROPERTY] === undefined) { element.dataset[COLLAPSED_ORDER_PROPERTY] = String(nextCollapsedOrder++); }\n  });\n  collapsed.sort((left, right) => Number(left.dataset[COLLAPSED_ORDER_PROPERTY]) - Number(right.dataset[COLLAPSED_ORDER_PROPERTY]));\n  expanded.forEach((element) => {\n    const collapsedLeft = element.style.getPropertyValue(COLLAPSED_LEFT_PROPERTY);\n    const collapsedTop = element.style.getPropertyValue(COLLAPSED_TOP_PROPERTY);\n    element.style.removeProperty(COLLAPSED_LEFT_PROPERTY);\n    element.style.removeProperty(COLLAPSED_TOP_PROPERTY);\n    if (collapsedLeft || collapsedTop) {\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window stack: released collapsed docking\", { windowKey: windowKey(element), collapsedLeft, collapsedTop, inlineLeft: element.style.left, inlineTop: element.style.top, inlineWidth: element.style.width, inlineHeight: element.style.height });\n    }\n  });\n  const width = FOBLES_WINDOW_COLLAPSED_WIDTH;\n  const gap = 4;\n  const availableWidth = Math.max(width, window.innerWidth - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN * 2);\n  const columns = Math.max(1, Math.floor((availableWidth + gap) / (width + gap)));\n  collapsed.forEach((element, index) => {\n    const column = index % columns;\n    const row = Math.floor(index / columns);\n    const collapsedLeft = `${FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN + column * (width + gap)}px`;\n    const collapsedTop = `${window.innerHeight - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN - (row + 1) * FOBLES_WINDOW_TITLE_BAR_HEIGHT - row * gap}px`;\n    element.style.setProperty(COLLAPSED_LEFT_PROPERTY, collapsedLeft);\n    element.style.setProperty(COLLAPSED_TOP_PROPERTY, collapsedTop);\n    extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window stack: applied collapsed docking\", { windowKey: windowKey(element), collapsedOrder: element.dataset[COLLAPSED_ORDER_PROPERTY], collapsedLeft, collapsedTop, inlineLeft: element.style.left, inlineTop: element.style.top, inlineWidth: element.style.width, inlineHeight: element.style.height });\n  });\n}\n\nexport function syncFoblesWindowStack(): void {\n  const windows = mountedWindows().sort((a, b) => currentZIndex(a) - currentZIndex(b));\n  normalize(windows);\n}\n\nexport function bringFoblesWindowToFront(element: HTMLElement): void {\n  const windows = mountedWindows().sort((a, b) => currentZIndex(a) - currentZIndex(b)).filter((candidate) => candidate !== element);\n  windows.push(element);\n  normalize(windows);\n  persistOrder(windows);\n}\n\nexport function registerFoblesWindow(element: HTMLElement): () => void {\n  let active = true;\n  void getStorageValue<unknown>([STACK_STORAGE_KEY], \"local\").then((values) => {\n    if (!active) { return; }\n    const stored = values[STACK_STORAGE_KEY];\n    const persistedOrder = stored && typeof stored === \"object\" && Array.isArray((stored as PersistedWindowStack).order) ? (stored as PersistedWindowStack).order : [];\n    const rank = new Map(persistedOrder.map((key, index) => [key, index]));\n    const windows = mountedWindows().sort((a, b) => (rank.get(windowKey(a)) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER) - (rank.get(windowKey(b)) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER));\n    normalize(windows);\n  });\n  bringFoblesWindowToFront(element);\n  return () => { active = false; queueMicrotask(syncFoblesWindowStack); };\n}\n"

      "content": "// @source-path [fobles] src/content/shared/dialog/use-fobles-window-geometry.ts\n\nimport { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from \"react\";\nimport {  FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN,\n} from \"./fobles-window.const\";\nimport {\n  centeredRect,\n  clampRect,\n  parsePersistedFoblesWindowState,\n  readRect,\n  viewportRect,\n} from \"./fobles-window-geometry-math\";\nimport type {\n  FoblesWindowGeometry,\n  FoblesWindowGeometryOptions,\n  FoblesWindowState,\n  ResizeEdge,\n  WindowRect,\n} from \"./fobles-window.types\";\n\nexport function useFoblesWindowGeometry({\n  storageKey,\n  defaultWidth,\n  defaultHeight,\n  minWidth,\n  minHeight,\n}: FoblesWindowGeometryOptions): FoblesWindowGeometry {\n  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);\n  const [state, setState] = useState<FoblesWindowState>(() => ({\n    type: FOBLES_WINDOW_STATE_TYPE,\n    ...centeredRect(window, defaultWidth, defaultHeight, minWidth, minHeight),\n    collapsed: false,\n    maximized: false,\n  }));\n  const [dragging, setDragging] = useState(false);\n  const stateRef = useRef(state);\n  stateRef.current = state;\n  // Set the moment the user first drags/resizes/collapses/maximizes, so a still-in-flight\n  // storage hydration can no longer stomp a newer in-session interaction once it resolves.\n  const hasUserInteractedRef = useRef(false);\n\n  const persist = useCallback(\n    (next: FoblesWindowState) => {\n      void setStorageValue({ [storageKey]: next }, \"local\");\n    },\n    [storageKey],\n  );\n\n  const restoreRectOrDefault = useCallback(\n    (current: FoblesWindowState): WindowRect =>\n      current.restoreRect ?? centeredRect(window, defaultWidth, defaultHeight, minWidth, minHeight),\n    [defaultWidth, defaultHeight, minWidth, minHeight],\n  );\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    let active = true;\n    void getStorageValue<unknown>([storageKey], \"local\").then((values) => {\n      if (!active || hasUserInteractedRef.current) {\n        return;\n      }\n      const hydrated = parsePersistedFoblesWindowState(values[storageKey], window, minWidth, minHeight);\n      if (hydrated) {\n        setState((current) => ({ ...current, ...hydrated }));\n      }\n    });\n    return () => {\n      active = false;\n    };\n  }, [storageKey, minWidth, minHeight]);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    function onViewportResize(): void {\n      setState((current) => {\n        if (current.collapsed) { return current; }\n        const next: FoblesWindowState = { ...current, ...clampRect(window, current, minWidth, minHeight) };\n        persist(next);\n        return next;\n      });\n    }\n    window.addEventListener(\"resize\", onViewportResize);\n    return () => window.removeEventListener(\"resize\", onViewportResize);\n  }, [minWidth, minHeight, persist]);\n\n  const toggleCollapse = useCallback(() => {\n    hasUserInteractedRef.current = true;\n    setState((current) => {\n      if (current.collapsed) {\n        const restoreRect = restoreRectOrDefault(current);\n        const next: FoblesWindowState = { ...current, ...restoreRect, collapsed: false, maximized: false, restoreRect: undefined };\n        extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: restoring minimized window\", { storageKey, current, restoreRect, next, domRect: dialogRef.current ? readRect(dialogRef.current) : undefined });\n        persist(next);\n        return next;\n      }\n      const restoreRect = { left: current.left, top: current.top, width: current.width, height: current.height };\n      const dockedTop = viewportRect(window).height - FOBLES_WINDOW_TITLE_BAR_HEIGHT - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN;\n      const next: FoblesWindowState = { ...current, restoreRect, collapsed: true, maximized: false, top: dockedTop };\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: minimizing window\", { storageKey, current, domRect: dialogRef.current ? readRect(dialogRef.current) : undefined, restoreRect, dockedTop, next });\n      persist(next);\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, [persist, restoreRectOrDefault, storageKey]);\n\n  const toggleMaximize = useCallback(() => {\n    hasUserInteractedRef.current = true;\n    setState((current) => {\n      if (current.maximized) {\n        const next: FoblesWindowState = {\n          ...current,\n          ...restoreRectOrDefault(current),\n          collapsed: false,\n          maximized: false,\n          restoreRect: undefined,\n        };\n        persist(next);\n        return next;\n      }\n      const viewport = viewportRect(window);\n      const restoreRect =\n FoblesWindowState = {\n        ...current,\n        restoreRect,\n        collapsed: false,\n        maximized: true,\n        left: FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN,\n        top: persist(next);\n      return next;\n    });\n  }, [persist, restoreRectOrDefault]);\n\n  const dragStartRef = useRef<{\n    pointerId: number;\n    clientX: number;\n    clientY: number;\n    left: (event.target instanceof Element && event.target.closest(\"button\"))) {\n      return;\n    }\n    if (stateRef.current.collapsed) {\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: ignored title pointer down while minimized\", { storageKey, pointerId: event.pointerId, detail: event.detail, state: stateRef.current });\n      return;\n    }\n    event.preventDefault();\n    const rect = dialogRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect();\n    if (!rect) {\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: title pointer down had no window rect\", { storageKey, pointerId: event.pointerId });\n      return;\n    }\n    hasUserInteractedRef.current = true;\n    dragStartRef.current = { pointerId: event.pointerId, clientX: event.clientX, clientY: event.clientY, left: rect.left, top: rect.top, moved: false };\n    event.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(event.pointerId);\n    setDragging(true);\n    extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: title drag armed\", { storageKey, pointerId: event.pointerId, detail: event.detail, rect: { left: rect.left, top: rect.top, width: rect.width, height: rect.height } });\n  }, [storageKey]);\n\n  const onTitlePointerMove = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n    const start = dragStartRef.current;\n    if (!start || start.pointerId !== event.pointerId) {\n      return;\n    }\n    const rectWidth = dialogRef.current?.getBoundingClientRect().width ?? 0;\n    const maxLeft = Math.max(\n      FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN,\n      window.innerWidth - rectWidth - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN,\n    );\n    const maxTop = Math.max(\n      FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN,\n      window.innerHeight - FOBLES_WINDOW_TITLE_BAR_HEIGHT - FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN,\n    );\n    const left = FOBLES_WINDOW_VIEWPORT_MARGIN), maxTop);\n    start.moved = start.moved || left !== start.left || top !== start.top;\n    setState((current) => ({ ...current, left, top }));\n  }, []);\n\n  const onTitlePointerUp = useCallback(\n    (event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n      const start = dragStartRef.current;\n      if (!start || start.pointerId !== event.pointerId) {\n        return;\n      }\n      event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);\n      dragStartRef.current = null;\n      setDragging(false);\n      if (!start.moved) {\n        extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: title click completed without geometry change\", { storageKey, pointerId: event.pointerId, detail: event.detail, state: stateRef.current });\n        return;\n      }\n      const next: FoblesWindowState = { ...stateRef.current, collapsed: false, maximized: false, restoreRect: undefined };\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Fobles window: title drag completed\", { storageKey, pointerId: event.pointerId, start, next });\n      setState(next);\n      persist(next);\n    },\n    [persist],\n  );\n\n  const resizeStartRef = useRef<{\n    pointerId: number;\n    edge: ResizeEdge;\n    startX: number;\n    startY: number;\n    rect: WindowRect;\n  } | null>(null);\n\n  const onResizePointerDown = useCallback((event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>, edge: ResizeEdge) => {\n    if (event.button !== 0) {\n      return;\n    }\n    event.preventDefault();\n    const dialog = dialogRef.current;\n    if (!dialog) {\n      return;\n    }\n    hasUserInteractedRef.current = true;\n    resizeStartRef.current = {\n      pointerId: event.pointerId,\n      edge,\n      startX: event.clientX,\n      startY: event.clientY,\n      rect:     const start = resizeStartRef.current;\n      if (!start || start.pointerId !== event.pointerId) {\n        return;\n      }\n      const dx = event.clientX - start.startX;\n      const dy = event.clientY - start.startY;\n      let next = { ...start.rect };\n      if (start.edge.includes(\"e\")) {\n        next.width = start.rect.width + dx;\n      }\n      if (start.edge.includes(\"s\")) {\n        next.height = start.rect.height + dy;\n      }\n      if (start.edge.includes(\"w\")) {\n        next.left = start.rect.left + dx;\n        next.width = start.rect.width - dx;\n      }\n      if (start.edge.includes(\"n\")) {\n        next.top = start.rect.top + dy;\n        next.height = start.rect.height - dy;\n      }\n      next = clampRect(window, next, minWidth, minHeight);\n      setState((current) => ({ ...current, ...next, maximized: false, collapsed: false, restoreRect: undefined }));\n    },\n    [minWidth, minHeight],\n  );\n\n  const onResizePointerUp = useCallback(\n    (event: React.PointerEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {\n      const start = resizeStartRef.current;\n      if (!start || start.pointerId !== event.pointerId) {\n        return;\n      }\n      event.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(event.pointerId);\n      resizeStartRef.current = null;\n      persist(stateRef.current);\n    },\n    [persist],\n  );\n\n  const style = string;\n  provenance: string;\n  childCount: number;\n};\n\nfunction itemDetailRows(treeNode: Extract<ReferenceModelNode, { nodeType: \"item\" }>): DetailRow[] {\n  const rows: DetailRow[] = provenance.section.name]];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"section-field\") {\n    rows = [\n      [\"Field Name\", provenance.fieldName],\n      [\"Field Value\", provenance.fieldValue],\n    ];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"field-reference\") {\n    rows = [\n      [\"Section\", provenance.sectionName],\n      [\"Field Name\", provenance.fieldName],\n      [\"Confidence\", = [[\"Device\", provenance.device.scDeviceItem.scItemName ?? provenance.device.scDeviceItem.scItemId ?? \"\"]];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"rendering-control\") {\n    rows = [[\"Rendering UID\", provenance.control.uid ?? \"\"]];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"rendering-parameter\") {\n    rows = [\n      [\"Parameter Name\", provenance.parameterName],\n      [\"Parameter Value\", provenance.parameterValue],\n      [\"Decoded Value\", provenance.decodedValue],\n    ];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"placeholder\") {\n    rows = [[\"Placeholder Path\", provenance.placeholderPath]];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"layout-xml\") {\n    rows = [[\"Layout\", provenance.label]];\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"synthetic\") {\n    rows = [[\"Synthetic\", provenance.label]];\n  }\n  return rows.filter(([, value]) => Boolean(value));\n}\n\nfunction resolveRawSource(treeNode: ReferenceModelNode): string | undefined {\n  const provenance = treeNode.provenance;\n  let rawSource: string | undefined;\n  if (provenance.type === \"sitecore-item\") {\n    rawSource = provenance.rawData\n      ? null,\n      2,\n    );\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"placeholder\") {\n    rawSource = JSON.stringify(provenance.rawData, null, 2);\n  } else if (provenance.type === \"layout-xml\") {\n    rawSource = provenance.rawMarkup;\n  }\n  return rawSource;\n}\n\nfunction resolveItemRawMarkup(treeNode: ReferenceModelNode): string | undefined {\n  let rawMarkup: string | undefined;\n  if prettier.format(source, { parser: \"html\", plugins: [prettierPluginHtml], printWidth: 120 });\n  return formatted.trim();\n}\n\nasync function prettifyRawSource(source: string): Promise<string> {\n  let formatted = source;\n  if (source.trimStart().startsWith(\"<\")) {\n    formatted = await prettifyHtml(source);\n  }\n  return formatted;\n}\n\nfunction buildDiagnosticTreeNode(treeNode: ReferenceModelNode): DiagnosticReferenceModelNode {\n  const { children, provenance: _provenance, ...node } = treeNode;\n  let diagnosticNode: DiagnosticReferenceModelNode;\n  if (treeNode.nodeType === \"item\") {\n    const { rawMarkup, ...scItem } = treeNode.scItem;\n    diagnosticNode = {\n      ...node,\n      scItem: {\n        ...scItem,\n        rawMarkup: rawMarkup ? \"[omitted from diagnostic view; see Item Raw Markup below]\" : undefined,\n      },\n      children: `[omitted from diagnostic view; ${children.length} child node(s)]`,\n      provenance: \"[omitted from diagnostic view; see Raw Source below]\",\n      childCount: children.length,\n    };\n  } else {\n    diagnosticNode = {\n      ...node,\n      children: `[omitted from diagnostic view; ${children.length} child node(s)]`,\n      provenance: \"[omitted from diagnostic view; see Raw Source below]\",\n      childCount: children.length,\n    };\n  }\n  return diagnosticNode;\n}\n\nfunction buildClipboardText(\n  treeNode: DiagnosticReferenceModelNode,\n  rawSource: string | undefined,\n  itemRawMarkup: string | undefined,\n): string {\n  return [\n    \"Reference Model Node\",\n    JSON.stringify(treeNode, null, 2),\n    \"\",\n    \"Raw Source\",\n    rawSource ?? \"\",\n    \"\",\n    \"Item Raw Markup\",\n    itemRawMarkup ?? \"\",\n  ].join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\nexport function NodeDetailsPanel({\n  selectedTreeNodeId,\n  nodesById,\n  processingStateByTreeNodeId,\n  onClose,\n}: NodeDetailsPanelProps) {\n  const treeNode = selectedTreeNodeId ? nodesById.get(selectedTreeNodeId) : undefined;\n  const [formattedRawSource, setFormattedRawSource] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);\n  const [formattedItemRawMarkup, setFormattedItemRawMarkup] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);\n  const rawSource = treeNode ? resolveRawSource(treeNode) : undefined;\n  const itemRawMarkup = treeNode ? resolveItemRawMarkup(treeNode) : undefined;\n  useEffect((): (() => void) => {\n    let disposed = false;\n    setFormattedRawSource(rawSource);\n    setFormattedItemRawMarkup(itemRawMarkup);\n    if (rawSource) {\n      void prettifyRawSource(rawSource)\n        .then((formatted): void => {\n          if (!disposed) {\n            setFormattedRawSource(formatted);\n          }\n        })\n        .catch((): void => {\n          if (!disposed) {\n            setFormattedRawSource(rawSource);\n          }\n        });\n    }\n    if (itemRawMarkup) {\n      void prettifyHtml(itemRawMarkup)\n        .then((formatted): void => {\n          if (!disposed) {\n            setFormattedItemRawMarkup(formatted);\n          }\n        })\n        .catch((): void => {\n          if (!disposed) {\n            setFormattedItemRawMarkup(itemRawMarkup);\n          }\n        });\n    }\n    return (): void => {\n      disposed = true;\n    };\n  }, [rawSource, itemRawMarkup]);\n  if (!treeNode) {\n    return null;\n  }\n  const rows: DetailRow[] =\n    treeNode.nodeType === \"item\"\n      ? [\n          ...itemDetailRows(treeNode),\n          ...(processingStateByTreeNodeId.get(treeNode.treeNodeId)\n            ?       "encoding": "utf8",
      "content": "// @source-path [fobles] src/content/shared/ui/indexeddb-viewer/indexeddb-viewer-app.tsx\nimport { useCallback, useDeferredValue, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { FoblesDetailsWindow } from \"../../dialog/fobles-details-window\";\nimport { DetailsPanel } from \"../details/details-panel\";\nimport { DETAILS_PANEL } from \"../details/details-panel.const\";\nimport { JsonTree } from \"../json-tree/json-tree\";\nimport { JSON_TREE } from \"../json-tree/json-tree.const\";\nimport { INDEXEDDB_VIEWER } from \"./indexeddb-viewer.const\";\nimport { clearSitecoreDataCache, filterCacheRows, loadCacheRows } from \"./indexeddb-viewer-service\";\nimport type { CacheRow, CacheRowsByStore, IndexedDbStoreId, IndexedDbViewerAppProps } from \"./indexeddb-viewer.types\";\nconst { CLASS, TEXT } = INDEXEDDB_VIEWER;\nconst EMPTY_ROWS: CacheRowsByStore = { sitecoreItems: [], sitecoreFacts: [] };\n\nexport function IndexedDbViewerApp({ doc }: IndexedDbViewerAppProps) {\n  const appRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);\n  const [rowsByStore, setRowsByStore] = useState<CacheRowsByStore>(EMPTY_ROWS);\n  const [activeStore, setActiveStore] = useState<IndexedDbStoreId>(\"sitecoreItems\");\n  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);\n  const [error, setError] = useState<string>();\n  const [query, setQuery] = useState(\"\");\n  const [selectedKey, setSelectedKey] = useState<string>();\n  const [openDepth, setOpenDepth] = useState<number>(JSON_TREE.DEFAULT_OPEN_DEPTH);\n  const [treeVersion, setTreeVersion] = useState(0);\n  const deferredQuery = useDeferredValue(query);\n  const win = doc.defaultView ?? window;\n  const reload = useCallback(async (): Promise<void> => {\n    try {\n      setRowsByStore(await loadCacheRows());\n      setError(undefined);\n    } catch (loadError) {\n      setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : String(loadError));\n    } finally {\n      setLoaded(true);\n    }\n  }, []);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void reload();\n  }, [reload]);\n  const rows = rowsByStore[activeStore];\n  const matches = useMemo(() => filterCacheRows(rows, deferredQuery), [rows, deferredQuery]);\n  const shown = useMemo(() => matches.slice(0, INDEXEDDB_VIEWER.MAX_ROWS), [matches]);\n  const selected = useMemo(() => rows.find((row) => row.key === selectedKey), [rows, selectedKey]);\n  function selectStore(store: IndexedDbStoreId): void {\n    setActiveStore(store);\n    setSelectedKey(undefined);\n  }\n  function selectRow(row: CacheRow): void {\n    setSelectedKey(row.key);\n    setOpenDepth(JSON_TREE.DEFAULT_OPEN_DEPTH);\n    setTreeVersion((current) => current + 1);\n  }\n  function setTreeDepth(depth: number): void {\n    setOpenDepth(depth);\n    setTreeVersion((current) => current + 1);\n  }\n  async function handleClear(): Promise<void> {\n    if (win.confirm(TEXT.CONFIRM_CLEAR)) {\n      await clearSitecoreDataCache();\n      setSelectedKey(undefined);\n      await reload();\n    }\n  }\n  let status: string;\n  if (error) {\n    status = `${TEXT.LOAD_FAILED}: ${error}`;\n  } else if (!loaded) {\n    status = TEXT.LOADING;\n  } else {\n    status = `${matches.length} of ${rows.length} records match. Showing ${shown.length}.`;\n  }\n  return (\n    <div className={CLASS.APP}>\n      <div className={CLASS.TABS} role=\"tablist\" aria-label={TEXT.STORE_TABS_LABEL}>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          role=\"tab\"\n          aria-selected={activeStore === \"sitecoreItems\"}\n          className={activeStore === \"sitecoreItems\" ? CLASS.ACTIVE_TAB : undefined}\n          onClick={() => selectStore(\"sitecoreItems\")}\n        >\n          {TEXT.SITECORE_ITEMS}\n        </button>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          role=\"tab\"\n          aria-selected={activeStore === \"sitecoreFacts\"}\n          className={activeStore === \"sitecoreFacts\" ? CLASS.ACTIVE_TAB : undefined}\n          onClick={() => selectStore(\"sitecoreFacts\")}\n        >\n          {TEXT.SITECORE_FACTS}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className={CLASS.TOOLBAR}>\n        <input\n          type=\"search\"\n          placeholder={TEXT.SEARCH_PLACEHOLDER}\n          aria-label={TEXT.SEARCH_LABEL}\n          value={query}\n          onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}\n        />\n        <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => void reload()}>\n          {TEXT.REFRESH}\n        </button>\n        <button type=\"button\" className={CLASS.DANGER} onClick={() => void handleClear()}>\n          {TEXT.CLEAR}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      <div className={CLASS.STATUS}>{status}</div>\n      <div className={CLASS.WORKSPACE}>\n        <div className={CLASS.TABLE_WRAP}>\n          {loaded && shown.length === 0 ?       "encoding": "utf8",
      "content": "// @source-path [fobles] src/content/shared/ui/indexeddb-viewer/runtime-settings-editor-service.ts\nimport { getStorageValue, setStorageValue } from \"../../../../shared/storage/storage.ts\";\nimport { normalizeRuntimeSettings } from \"../../../runtime-settings/runtime-settings.ts\";\nimport { RUNTIME_SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY } from \"../../../runtime-settings/runtime-settings.const.ts\";\nimport type { RuntimeSettingsSaveResult } from \"./developer-dialog.types.ts\";\nconst SAVED = \"Saved.\";\nconst SAVED_NORMALIZED = \"Saved. Unknown keys were dropped and missing or invalid values were reset to defaults.\";\nconst NOT_AN_OBJECT = \"Settings must be a JSON object.\";\nconst NOT_PERSISTED =\n  \"Save failed: the value was not persisted. The extension context may have been reloaded - refresh the page.\";\nfunction prettify(value: unknown): string {\n  return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);\n}\n// Key-order-insensitive form, so reordering keys is not reported as a change.\nfunction canonical(value: unknown): string {\n  return JSON.stringify(value, (_key: string, nested: unknown) =>\n    nested && typeof nested === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(nested)\n      ? Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(nested).sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right)))\n      : nested,\n  );\n}\nfunction parseJson(text: string): { ok: true; value: unknown } | { ok: false; error: string } {\n  let parsed: { ok: true; value: unknown } | { ok: false; error: string };\n  try {\n    parsed = { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(text) as unknown };\n  } catch (error) {\n    parsed = { ok: false, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };\n  }\n  return parsed;\n}\nasync function readStored(): Promise<unknown> {\n  return (await getStorageValue<unknown>([RUNTIME_SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY], \"local\"))[RUNTIME_SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY];\n}\n\n// Shows the stored value normalized, so keys added since it was persisted (and defaults, when nothing is stored yet) are visible and editable.\nexport async function loadRuntimeSettingsText(): Promise<string> {\n  return prettify(normalizeRuntimeSettings(await readStored()));\n}\n\n// The value stays in the same storage key and area; it is normalized with the same rules the runtime applies on load, written, then read back to prove it persisted.\nexport async function saveRuntimeSettingsText(text: string): Promise<RuntimeSettingsSaveResult> {\n  const parsed = parseJson(text);\n  let result: RuntimeSettingsSaveResult;\n  if (!parsed.ok) {\n    result = { ok: false, message: `Invalid JSON: ${parsed.error}` };\n  } else if (parsed.value === null || typeof parsed.value !== \"object\" || Array.isArray(parsed.value)) {\n    result = { ok: false, message: NOT_AN_OBJECT };\n  } else {\n    const normalized = normalizeRuntimeSettings(parsed.value);\n    await setStorageValue({ [RUNTIME_SETTINGS_STORAGE_KEY]: normalized }, \"local\");\n    const stored = await readStored();\n    if (stored === undefined || canonical(stored) !== canonical(normalized)) {\n      result = { ok: false, message: NOT_PERSISTED };\n    } else {\n      result = {\n        ok: true,\n        message: canonical(parsed.value) === canonical(normalized) ? SAVED : SAVED_NORMALIZED,\n        text: prettify(normalized),\n      };\n    }\n  }\n  return result;\n}\n"

setSavedText(result.text);\n    }\n    setStatus({ ok: result.ok, message: result.message });\n    setSaving(false);\n  }\n  function handleChange(value: string): void {\n    setText(value);\n    setStatus(undefined);\n  }\n  let statusText = \"\";\n  if (!loaded) {\n    statusText = TEXT.LOADING;\n  } else if (status) {\n    statusText = status.message;\n  } else if (dirty) {\n    statusText !foblesNavVisible;\n      extensionLog.info(\"Hotkey triggered: toggle-fobles (nav visibility)\", { nextVisible });\n      void setFoblesNavVisible(nextVisible);\n    } else if (request.action === changes[STORAGE.KEY.SHOW_RELOAD_EXTENSION_BUTTON]) {\n      void reconcileCurrentPage();\n    }\n    if (!pageEligible) {\n      return;\n    }\n    const change = changes[STORAGE.KEY.FOBLES_NAV_VISIBLE];\n    if (typeof change?.newValue === \"boolean\") {\n      setToolbarVisible(getToolbarContext(), change.newValue);\n    }\n    if (!currentAllowedPage) {\n      return;\n    }\n    const positionKey = `${STORAGE.KEY.FOBLES_NAV_POSITION}_${currentAllowedPage.id}`;\n    const placement = changes[positionKey]?.newValue as ToolbarPlacement | undefined;\n    if       "encoding": "utf8",
      "content": "# Toolbar worker diagnostics\n\n## Agents: contracts\n\n- Inline in ToolbarApp; reuse PhaseProgressBar, never open a separate window. History and Options are independent.\n- Diagnostics occupy the second grid row beneath the toolbar buttons inside the same draggable container, including bottom placements; never position them absolutely or flip them above the buttons. Inline-size containment keeps diagnostic text from widening the button row. Toolbar/visible worker stacking uses the shared Sass tokens (9999/9998 respectively).\n- Structural sync setting `showToolbarDiagnostics` defaults to false. Observe settings live; subscribe/read pool status only while enabled and dispose subscriptions on disable/unmount.\n- Initial getWorkerPoolStatus establishes the host tab. Accept only unfiltered snapshots for that numeric host; ignore undefined/different hosts and request-filtered snapshots. Subscribe before requesting the initial snapshot so matching live updates win the initial-read race.\n- Show active/max workers, remaining capacity, foreground/background active/queued counts, and the foreground-first current operation, requested item (URL fallback), and scheduler phase. Worker detail events require both request and task identity from the accepted host snapshot.\n- Focused Node rendering, subscription, and in-memory Sass layout/stacking tests live in tests/unit/toolbar-diagnostics; no live browser tests are required."

      "path": "files/src/content/toolbar/diagnostics/toolbar-diagnostics-subscription.ts",
      "encoding": "utf8",
      "content": "import type { ActivityEvent } from \"../../shared/activity/activity.types\";\nimport type { WorkerPoolStatus } from \"../../foundation/workers/pool/worker-pool-status.types\";\nimport type { ToolbarDiagnosticsServices, ToolbarDiagnosticsState } from \"./toolbar-diagnostics.types\";\n\nexport function observeToolbarDiagnostics(\n  services: ToolbarDiagnosticsServices,\n  onChange: (state: ToolbarDiagnosticsState) => void,\n): () => void {\n  let disposed = false;\n  let settingsVersion = 0;\n  let generation = 0;\n  let state: ToolbarDiagnosticsState = { enabled: false };\n  let stopActivity: (() => void) | undefined;\n\n  function publish(next: ToolbarDiagnosticsState): void {\n    state = next;\n    onChange(state);\n  }\n\n  function applyEnabled(enabled: boolean): void {\n    if (!disposed && enabled !== state.enabled) {\n      const currentGeneration = ++generation;\n      stopActivity?.();\n      stopActivity = undefined;\n      publish({ enabled });\n      if (enabled) {\n        let hostTabId: number | undefined;\n        const pending = new Map<number, WorkerPoolStatus>();\n        stopActivity = services.subscribe((activity: ActivityEvent) => {\n          if (!disposed && generation === currentGeneration) {\n            const status = activity.poolStatus;\n            if (status && status.requestId === undefined && typeof status.hostTabId === \"number\") {\n              if (hostTabId === undefined) {\n                pending.set(status.hostTabId, status);\n              } else if (status.hostTabId === hostTabId) {\n                publish({ enabled: true, status });\n              }\n            } else if (!status && state.status?.activeTasks.some((task) =>\n              task.requestId === activity.requestId && task.taskId === activity.activityId)) {\n              publish({ ...state, activity });\n            }\n          }\n        });\n        void services.getStatus().then((status) => {\n          if (!disposed && generation === currentGeneration && typeof status?.hostTabId === \"number\" &&\n            status.requestId === undefined) {\n            hostTabId = status.hostTabId;\n            publish({ enabled: true, status: pending.get(hostTabId) ?? status });\n          }\n          pending.clear();\n        }).catch(() => pending.clear());\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  const stopSettings = services.watchSettings((settings) => {\n    settingsVersion++;\n    applyEnabled(settings.showToolbarDiagnostics === true);\n  });\n  const initialVersion = settingsVersion;\n  void services.getSettings().then((settings) => {\n    if (settingsVersion === initialVersion) {\n      applyEnabled(settings.showToolbarDiagnostics === true);\n    }\n  }).catch(() => undefined);\n\n  return () => {\n    disposed = true;\n    generation++;\n    stopActivity?.();\n    stopSettings();\n  };\n}"

      "content": "// @source-path [fobles] src/content/toolbar/diagnostics/worker-task-manager-model.ts\nimport type { ActivityEvent } from \"../../shared/activity/activity-events\";\nimport type { WorkerPoolStatus } from \"../../foundation/workers/pool/worker-pool-status.types\";\n\nexport type WorkerTaskManagerRow = {\n  id: string;\n  activityAt: number;\n  worker: string;\n  status: string;\n  task: string;\n  target: string;\n  workClass: string;\n  phase: string;\n  outcome: string;\n  recoveries: number;\n};\n\nexport const WORKER_TASK_MANAGER_HISTORY_MAX = 100;\n\nfunction outcomeFor(activity: ActivityEvent): string {\n  if (activity.state === \"completed\") { return activity.detail === \"success\" ? \"Success\" : \"Completed\"; }\n  if (activity.state === \"error\") { return \"Error\"; }\n  if (activity.state === \"cancelled\") { return \"Cancelled\"; }\n  return \"—\";\n}\n\nfunction phaseFor(activity: ActivityEvent): string {\n  if (activity.state === \"started\") { return \"Executing\"; }\n  if (activity.state === \"completed\") { return \"Completed\"; }\n  if (activity.state === \"error\") { return \"Failed\"; }\n  if (activity.state === \"cancelled\") { return \"Cancelled\"; }\n  return activity.state;\n}\n\nexport function updateRowsFromActivity(rows: readonly WorkerTaskManagerRow[], activity: ActivityEvent): WorkerTaskManagerRow[] {\n  if (activity.source !== \"worker\" || !activity.activityId) { return [...rows]; }\n  const old = rows.find((row) => row.id === activity.activityId);\n  const next: WorkerTaskManagerRow = {\n    id: activity.activityId,\n    activityAt: activity.timestamp,\n    worker: activity.detail?.startsWith(\"iframe:\") || activity.detail?.startsWith(\"tab:\") ? activity.detail : old?.worker ?? \"—\",\n    status: activity.state === \"started\" ? \"Active\" : \"Disposed\",\n    task: activity.label,\n    target: old?.target ?? \"—\",\n    workClass: activity.workClass === \"foreground\" ? \"FG\" : activity.workClass === \"background\" ? \"BG\" : old?.workClass ?? \"—\",\n    phase: phaseFor(activity),\n    outcome: outcomeFor(activity),\n    recoveries: old?.recoveries ?? 0,\n  };\n  return [next, ...rows.filter((row) => row.id !== next.id)].slice(0, WORKER_TASK_MANAGER_HISTORY_MAX);\n}\n\nexport function reconcileActiveRows(rows: readonly WorkerTaskManagerRow[], status: WorkerPoolStatus | undefined, now = Date.now()): WorkerTaskManagerRow[] {\n  let next = [...rows];\n  for (const worker of status?.workers ?? []) {\n    const task = worker.activeTask;\n    if (!task) { continue; }\n    const existing = next.find((row) => row.id === task.taskId);\n    const reconciled: WorkerTaskManagerRow = {\n      id: task.taskId,\n      activityAt: existing?.activityAt ?? now,\n      worker: worker.targetKind ? `${worker.targetKind}:${worker.workerNumber}` : String(worker.workerNumber),\n      status: worker.state,\n      task: existing?.task ?? task.type,\n      target: task.item?.value ?? worker.currentUrl ?? existing?.target ?? \"—\",\n      workClass: task.workClass === \"foreground\" ? \"FG\" : \"BG\",\n      phase: existing?.phase ?? task.phase,\n      outcome: existing?.outcome ?? \"—\",\n      recoveries: worker.recoveryCount,\n    };\n    next = [reconciled, ...next.filter((row) => row.id !== reconciled.id)];\n  }\n  return next.slice(0, WORKER_TASK_MANAGER_HISTORY_MAX);\n}\n"

\"../../../constants/fobles.constants\";\nimport { FOBLES } from \"../../../constants/fobles\";\nimport { extensionLog } from \"../../logger\";\nimport { findRibbonCheckbox, postSitecoreEvent } from \"../../features/augmentor/proxy-buttons-ribbon\";\nimport { SC_PROXY_BUTTONS } from \"./proxy-flyout.const\";\n\nexport type ProxyFlyoutProps = {\n  doc: Document;\n  visible: boolean;\n  onCancelHide: () => void;\n  onScheduleHide: () => void;\n};\n\ntype ProxyButtonOption = (typeof SC_PROXY_BUTTONS)[number];\n\nfunction ProxyButtonCheckbox({ doc, option, visible }: { doc: Document; option: ProxyButtonOption; visible: boolean }) {\n  const checkboxRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Syncs the checkbox from the real ribbon checkbox (the source of truth) whenever this\n    // panel is opened, in addition to once at creation.\n    if (checkboxRef.current) {\n      checkboxRef.current.checked = Boolean(findRibbonCheckbox(doc, option.checkboxId)?.checked);\n    }\n  }, [doc, option.checkboxId, visible]);\n\n  function handleChange(): void {\n    const checkbox = checkboxRef.current;\n    const ribbonCheckbox = findRibbonCheckbox(doc, option.checkboxId);\n    extensionLog.debug(\"Proxy button click\", {\n      checkboxFound: !!ribbonCheckbox,\n      checkboxId: option.checkboxId,\n      checked: checkbox?.checked,\n      option: option.label,\n    });\n    if (ribbonCheckbox) {\n      ribbonCheckbox.click();\n      // Don't trust the browser's optimistic native toggle: reflect whatever Sitecore's\n      // real checkbox actually ended up at, in case its own click handling diverges.\n      if (checkbox) {\n        checkbox.checked = ribbonCheckbox.checked;\n      }\n    } else {\n      const posted = postSitecoreEvent(doc, option.eventName);\n      extensionLog.debug(\"Proxy button fallback\", { eventName: option.eventName, option: option.label, posted });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className={CLASS.PROXY_BUTTONS_ACTION}>\n      <label\n        className={`${CLASS.PROXY_BUTTON} ${CLASS.FOBLES_NAV_BUTTON} ${CLASS.FOBLES_NAV_BUTTON_COMPACT}`}\n        title={option.label}\n      >\n        <input\n          ref={checkboxRef}\n          type=\"checkbox\"\n          id={option.id}\n          className={CLASS.PROXY_BUTTON_INPUT}\n          {...{ [FOBLES.ATTRIBUTES.DATA.KEY.FOBLES_NAV_OWNER]: FOBLES.ATTRIBUTES.DATA.VALUE.PERSISTENT }}\n          onChange={handleChange}\n        />\n        <span className={CLASS.PROXY_BUTTON_LABEL}>{option.label}</span>\n      </label>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n\n// These live in the main toolbar (not the editor header) since the editor header\n// gets redrawn every time a tree item is picked. Proxy buttons mirror a real Sitecore ribbon\n// checkbox and proxy clicks to it; the ribbon checkbox stays the source of truth.\nexport function ProxyFlyout({ doc, visible, onCancelHide, onScheduleHide }: ProxyFlyoutProps) {\n  return (\n    <div\n      className={CLASS.PROXY_BUTTONS}\n      data-visible={visible ? \"true\" : \"false\"}\n      {...{ [FOBLES.ATTRIBUTES.DATA.KEY.PROXY_BUTTONS]: \"1\" }}\n      onMouseEnter={onCancelHide}\n      onMouseLeave={onScheduleHide}\n    >\n      <div className={CLASS.PROXY_BUTTONS_ACTIONS}>\n        {SC_PROXY_BUTTONS.map((option) => (\n          <ProxyButtonCheckbox key={option.id} doc={doc} option={option} visible={visible} />\n        ))}\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

className={drag.className}\n      title={TEXT.DRAG_NAV}\n      data-position={drag.dataPosition}\n      style={{ ...drag.style, ...backgroundStyle }}\n      onPointerDown={drag.onPointerDown}\n       "content": "// @source-path [fobles] src/options/jump-flyout-buttons/jump-flyout-buttons-section.tsx\n\nimport { useEffect, useState } from \"react\";\nimport { JUMP_FLYOUT_BUTTON_CATALOG, type JumpFlyoutButtonDescriptor } from \"../../shared/jump-flyout/button-catalog\";\nimport {\n  getJumpFlyoutButtonSettings,\n  sanitizeJumpFlyoutPathSuffix,\n  setJumpFlyoutButtonSettings,\n  type JumpFlyoutButtonSettings,\n} from \"../../shared/jump-flyout/button-settings\";\nimport {\n  getUserTreeJumps,\n  setUserTreeJumps,\n  type UserTreeJump,\n} from \"../../shared/jump-flyout/user-tree-jump-settings\";\nimport {\n  getUserAdminPages,\n  setUserAdminPages,\n  type UserAdminPage,\n} from \"../../shared/jump-flyout/user-admin-page-settings\";\nimport { TEXT } from \"../../constants/fobles.constants\";\nimport { JumpFlyoutColumn } from \"./jump-flyout-column\";\nimport { JumpFlyoutButtonRow } from \"./jump-flyout-button-row\";\nimport { UserTreeJumpEditor, normalizeUserTreeJumpEntriesForSave } from \"./user-tree-jump-editor\";\nimport { UserAdminPageEditor, normalizeUserAdminPageEntriesForSave } from \"./user-admin-page-editor\";\n\nfunction groupByColumn(descriptors: readonly JumpFlyoutButtonDescriptor[]): Map<string, JumpFlyoutButtonDescriptor[]> {\n  const columns = new Map<string, JumpFlyoutButtonDescriptor[]>();\n  descriptors.forEach((descriptor) => {\n    const column = columns.get(descriptor.column) ?? [];\n    column.push(descriptor);\n    columns.set(descriptor.column, column);\n  });\n  return columns;\n}\n\nconst COLUMNS = groupByColumn(JUMP_FLYOUT_BUTTON_CATALOG);\n\nexport function JumpFlyoutButtonsSection() {\n  const [enabledByButtonId, setEnabledByButtonId] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});\n  const [pathSuffixByButtonId, setPathSuffixByButtonId] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});\n  const [userTreeJumps, setUserTreeJumpsState] = useState<UserTreeJump[]>([]);\n  const [userAdminPages, setUserAdminPagesState] = useState<UserAdminPage[]>([]);\n  const [openColumn, setOpenColumn] = useState<string>();\n  const [status, setStatus] = useState(\"\");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    void Promise.all([getJumpFlyoutButtonSettings(), getUserTreeJumps(), getUserAdminPages()]).then(\n      ([settings, treeJumps, adminPages]) => {\n        const enabled: Record<string, boolean> = {};\n        const pathSuffix: Record<string, string> = {};\n        JUMP_FLYOUT_BUTTON_CATALOG.forEach((descriptor) => {\n          enabled[descriptor.id] = settings[descriptor.id]?.enabled !== false;\n          pathSuffix[descriptor.id] = settings[descriptor.id]?.encodedPathSuffix ?? \"\";\n        });\n        setEnabledByButtonId(enabled);\n        setPathSuffixByButtonId(pathSuffix);\n        setUserTreeJumpsState(treeJumps);\n        setUserAdminPagesState(adminPages);\n      },\n    );\n  }, []);\n\n  function sanitizePathSuffix(buttonId: string): void {\n    setPathSuffixByButtonId((current) => ({\n      ...current,\n      [buttonId]: sanitizeJumpFlyoutPathSuffix(current[buttonId] ?? \"\"),\n    }));\n  }\n\n  async function handleSave(): Promise<void> {\n    const settings: JumpFlyoutButtonSettings = {};\n    JUMP_FLYOUT_BUTTON_CATALOG.forEach((descriptor) => {\n      settings[descriptor.id] = {\n        label: descriptor.label,\n        enabled: enabledByButtonId[descriptor.id] !== false,\n        encodedPathSuffix: sanitizeJumpFlyoutPathSuffix(pathSuffixByButtonId[descriptor.id] ?? \"\"),\n      };\n    });\n    await Promise.all([\n      setJumpFlyoutButtonSettings(settings),\n      setUserTreeJumps(normalizeUserTreeJumpEntriesForSave(userTreeJumps)),\n      setUserAdminPages(normalizeUserAdminPageEntriesForSave(userAdminPages)),\n    ]);\n    setStatus(\"Jump flyout buttons saved.\");\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <details className=\"settings-section\">\n      <summary>Jump Menu Buttons</summary>\n      <p>\n        Toggle buttons on or off, and optionally add a sub-path suffix to a Tree Jump button's base path. Under Tree\n        Jumps, add your own User Tree Jumps too - each is rooted at /sitecore/, up to 30.\n      </p>\n      <div id=\"jump-flyout-buttons\">\n        {Array.from(COLUMNS.entries()).map(([columnTitle, descriptors]) => (\n          <JumpFlyoutColumn\n            key={columnTitle}\n            title={columnTitle}\n            open={openColumn === columnTitle}\n            onToggle={(open) => setOpenColumn(open ? columnTitle : undefined)}\n          >\n            {descriptors.map((descriptor) => (\n              <JumpFlyoutButtonRow\n                key={descriptor.id}\n                descriptor={descriptor}\n                enabled={enabledByButtonId[descriptor.id] !== false}\n                pathSuffix={pathSuffixByButtonId[descriptor.id] ?? \"\"}\n                onToggle={(enabled) => setEnabledByButtonId((current) => ({ ...current, [descriptor.id]: enabled }))}\n                onPathSuffixChange={(value) =>\n                  setPathSuffixByButtonId((current) => ({ ...current, [descriptor.id]: value }))\n                }\n                onPathSuffixBlur={() => sanitizePathSuffix(descriptor.id)}\n              />\n            ))}\n            {columnTitle === TEXT.GROUP_NAME.TREE_JUMPS && (\n              <UserTreeJumpEditor entries={userTreeJumps} onChange={setUserTreeJumpsState} />\n            )}\n            {columnTitle === TEXT.GROUP_NAME.ADMIN_PAGES && (\n              <UserAdminPageEditor entries={userAdminPages} onChange={setUserAdminPagesState} />\n            )}\n          </JumpFlyoutColumn>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <button id=\"save-jump-flyout-buttons\" type=\"button\" onClick={() => void handleSave()}>\n        Save jump flyout buttons\n      </button>\n      <p id=\"jump-flyout-buttons-status\" role=\"status\">\n        {status}\n      </p>\n    </details>\n  );\n}\n"
    },

normalizeUserAdminPageUrl,\n  type UserAdminPage,\n} from \"../../shared/jump-flyout/user-admin-page-settings\";\nimport { USER_ADMIN_PAGE } from \"../../shared/jump-flyout/user-admin-page-constants\";\n\nexport type UserAdminPageEditorProps = {\n  entries: readonly UserAdminPage[];\n  onChange: (entries: UserAdminPage[]) => void;\n};\n\nexport function createEmptyUserAdminPage(): UserAdminPage {\n  return { id: createUserAdminPageId(), label: \"\", enabled: true, icon: \"\", url: \"\" };\n}\n\n// Re-normalizes everything right before save, in case a row was never blurred (e.g. pasted then saved immediately).\nexport function normalizeUserAdminPageEntriesForSave(entries: readonly UserAdminPage[]): UserAdminPage[] {\n  return entries\n    .filter((entry) => entry.label.trim())\n    .map((entry) => ({\n      ...entry,\n      url: normalizeUserAdminPageUrl(entry.url),\n      icon: normalizeUserAdminPageIconPath(entry.icon),\n    }))\n    .slice(0, USER_ADMIN_PAGE.MAX_ENTRIES);\n}\n\nexport function UserAdminPageEditor({ entries, onChange }: UserAdminPageEditorProps) {\n  const atLimit = entries.length >= USER_ADMIN_PAGE.MAX_ENTRIES;\n\n  function updateEntry(id: string, patch: Partial<UserAdminPage>): void {\n    onChange(entries.map((entry) => (entry.id === id ? { ...entry, ...patch } : entry)));\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"user-admin-page-section\">\n      <div className=\"user-admin-page-heading\">User Admin Pages</div>\n      <div className=\"user-admin-page-rows\">\n        {entries.map((entry) => (\n          <div key={entry.id} className=\"jump-flyout-entry user-admin-page-row\" data-user-admin-page-id={entry.id}>\n            <div className=\"jump-flyout-entry-title\">\n              <input\n                type=\"text\"\n                name=\"label\"\n                placeholder={`e.g. ${USER_ADMIN_PAGE.EXAMPLE_LABEL}`}\n                value={entry.label}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { label: event.target.value })}\n              />\n              <button\n                type=\"button\"\n                className=\"user-admin-page-remove\"\n                title=\"Remove this Admin Page\"\n                onClick={() => onChange(entries.filter((other) => other.id !== entry.id))}\n              >\n                ×\n              </button>\n            </div>\n            <div className=\"jump-flyout-entry-fields\">\n              <input\n                type=\"checkbox\"\n                name=\"enabled\"\n                checked={entry.enabled}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { enabled: event.target.checked })}\n              />\n              <input\n                type=\"text\"\n                name=\"url\"\n                placeholder={`e.g. ${USER_ADMIN_PAGE.EXAMPLE_URL}`}\n                value={entry.url}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { url: event.target.value })}\n                onBlur={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { url: normalizeUserAdminPageUrl(event.target.value) })}\n              />\n              <input\n                type=\"text\"\n                name=\"icon\"\n                placeholder={USER_ADMIN_PAGE.DEFAULT_ICON_PATH}\n                title=\"A Sitecore icon path - normalized to the required format on save.\"\n                value={entry.icon}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { icon: event.target.value })}\n                onBlur={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { icon: normalizeUserAdminPageIconPath(event.target.value) })}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        ))}\n      </div>\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        className=\"user-admin-page-add\"\n        disabled={atLimit}\n        onClick={() => onChange([...entries, createEmptyUserAdminPage()])}\n      >\n        + Add User Admin Page\n      </button>\n      <p className=\"user-admin-page-limit-status\">\n        {atLimit ? `Maximum of ${USER_ADMIN_PAGE.MAX_ENTRIES} User Admin Pages reached.` : \"\"}\n      </p>\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"

name=\"enabled\"\n                checked={entry.enabled}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { enabled: event.target.checked })}\n              />\n              <span               value={entry.pathSuffix}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { pathSuffix: event.target.value })}\n                onBlur={(event) =>\n value={entry.icon}\n                onChange={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { icon: event.target.value })}\n                onBlur={(event) => updateEntry(entry.id, { icon:
